│    and command-line related tasks.                                           │
│  • You, all other agents and the user operate on the same system.            │
│  • Once the user provides a task, the Multi Agent System's goal is to        │
│    complete the task collaboratively, without returning to the user for      │
│    clarification.- The chat history includes the user's task, progress       │
│    summaries, and information from previous agents, such as previous         │

│                                                                              │
│                               Task Description                               │
│                                                                              │
│  • Role: you are the Explorer agent of the Multi Agent System.               │
│  • Based on the provided task by the user, use the given tools to gather     │
│    some basic information relevant to completing the task.                   │
│  • Do not try to solve the tasks, instead, collect only basic information    │
│    that will assist later agents in planning and actively solving the task.  │
│  • When finished write a concise summary of only the gathered information    │
│    and include the tag '[solver]'.                                           │
│  • Include a tag in your answer only when you intend to pass it on.          │

│                                                                              │
│                         Code Execution Capabilities                          │
│                                                                              │
│  • Your are equipped with different tools for executing code.                │
│  • Do only use the tools to execute code. Code inside a Markdown Code Block  │
│    will not be executed.                                                     │
│  • Use Shell tool for simple, single bash commands. Do not use it for        │
│    commands that open interactive terminal programs (e.g. git log or man).   │
│  • Use Terminal tool to execute commands in a persistent terminal instance   │
│    and get back the exact terminal screen. This is especially useful for     │
│    interactive programs such as git log, man, or vim.                        │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│                             More Operating Rules                             │
│                                                                              │

│  • Use tools to gather knowledge or verify assumptions before proceeding.    │
│  • If you call a tool it will be executed and results will be returned to    │
│    you.                                                                      │

│  • Your task is now to test if the overall user's task was completed         │
│    successfully.                                                             │

╭──────────────────── logging - approver - system_message ─────────────────────╮
│                                                                              │
│                                   Context                                    │
│                                                                              │
│  • You are a helpful agent part of a Multi Agent System that solves server   │
│    and command-line related tasks.                                           │
│  • You, all other agents and the user operate on the same system.            │
│  • Once the user provides a task, the Multi Agent System's goal is to        │
│    complete the task collaboratively, without returning to the user for      │
│    clarification.- The chat history includes the user's task, progress       │
│    summaries, and information from previous agents, such as previous         │
│    sub-task plans, feedback, executed steps, exploration details and more.   │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│                               Task Description                               │
│                                                                              │
│  • Your role is the "Approver" agent of the Multi Agent System.              │
│  • Your job is to determine if the agents have done enough work on the task. │
│  • Make your decision based on all information gathered and the actions      │
│    performed by previous agents.                                             │
│  • Be critical and selective, approve only what you are completely sure of.  │
│  • If you decide that the task was not solved successfully, briefly explain  │
│    why and include the tag '[solver]'.                                       │
│  • If you decide that not enough testing was done to ensure the task is      │
│    fully and successfully completed, briefly explain why and add the tag     │
│    '[tester]'.                                                               │
│  • If you decide that sufficient work was done, just reply briefly with the  │
│    tag '[summarizer]'.                                                       │
│  • Make one of the three decisions and include its tag in your response.     │

│ using hashing trick\nimport os,sys,math,random\nimport numpy as np\nfrom     │
│ collections import defaultdict\n\ntrain_file =                               │
│ &#x27;/tmp/ft_train.txt&#x27;\ntest_file =                                   │
│ &#x27;/tmp/ft_test.txt&#x27;\nmodel_path = &#x27;/app/model.bin&#x27;\n\n#   │
│ Hyperparams\nbuckets = 200000  # hashed vocabulary size\ndim = 50\nepochs =  │
│ 3\nlr = 0.05\nbatch_size = 512\nmax_train_lines = 200000  # sample from      │
│ training file\nmin_tokens = 1\nnum_classes = 5\nchar_ngrams =                │
│ (3,4,5)\n\nrandom.seed(42)\nnp.random.seed(42)\n\n# Utilities\ndef           │
│ hash_token(t):\n    return (hash(t) & 0xffffffff) % buckets\n\ndef           │
│ token_to_indices(token):\n    # word token\n    inds =                       │
│ [hash_token(&#x27;w:&#x27;+token)]\n    # char ngrams\n    s = &#x27;<&#x27; │
│ + token + &#x27;>&#x27;\n    L = len(s)\n    for n in char_ngrams:\n         │
│ if L <= n: continue\n        for i in range(L - n + 1):\n            ng =    │
│ s[i:i+n]\n            inds.append(hash_token(&#x27;g:&#x27;+ng))\n    return │
│ inds\n\ndef parse_line(line):\n    # format: label<label> <text>\\n\n    if  │
│ not line: return None\n    parts = line.split(&#x27; &#x27;,1)\n    if       │
│ len(parts) < 2: return None\n    lbl = parts[0].strip()\n    if not          │
│ lbl.startswith(&#x27;label&#x27;): return None\n    y =                      │
│ int(lbl.replace(&#x27;label&#x27;,&#x27;&#x27;))\n    text =                 │
│ parts[1].strip().lower()\n    tokens = text.split()\n    indices = []\n      │
│ for t in tokens:\n        t = t.strip()\n        if not t: continue\n        │
│ inds = token_to_indices(t)\n        indices.extend(inds)\n    if             │
│ len(indices) < min_tokens:\n        return None\n    return y, indices\n\n#  │
│ Read sample of training lines (first max_train_lines)\nprint(&#x27;Reading   │
│ up to&#x27;, max_train_lines, &#x27;train lines...&#x27;)\ntrain_samples =   │
│ []\nwith open(train_file, &#x27;r&#x27;, encoding=&#x27;utf-8&#x27;) as f:\n │
│ for i,line in enumerate(f):\n        if i >= max_train_lines: break\n        │
│ parsed = parse_line(line)\n        if parsed:\n                              │
│ train_samples.append(parsed)\n\nprint(&#x27;Loaded&#x27;,                    │
│ len(train_samples), &#x27;train samples&#x27;)\n\n# Read test set            │
│ fully\nprint(&#x27;Reading test set...&#x27;)\ntest_samples = []\nwith       │
│ open(test_file, &#x27;r&#x27;, encoding=&#x27;utf-8&#x27;) as f:\n    for    │

│ test_samples.append(parsed)\nprint(&#x27;Loaded&#x27;, len(test_samples),    │
│ &#x27;test samples&#x27;)\n\n# Initialize model\nW = np.zeros((num_classes,  │
│ dim), dtype=np.float32)  # classifier weights\nb = np.zeros((num_classes,),  │
│ dtype=np.float32)\nE = (np.random.randn(buckets, dim).astype(np.float32) *   │
│ 0.01)  # embeddings\n\n# Training\nprint(&#x27;Starting training             │
│ epochs=&#x27;, epochs, &#x27;buckets=&#x27;, buckets, &#x27;dim=&#x27;,      │
│ dim)\nfor epoch in range(epochs):\n    random.shuffle(train_samples)\n       │
│ total = 0\n    correct = 0\n    for i in range(0, len(train_samples),        │
│ batch_size):\n        batch = train_samples[i:i+batch_size]\n        #       │
│ process each sample in batch\n        for y, inds in batch:\n                │
│ total += 1\n            # compute averaged embedding\n            vec =      │
│ np.zeros((dim,), dtype=np.float32)\n            for idx in inds:\n           │
│ vec += E[idx]\n            vec /= max(1, len(inds))\n            # logits\n  │
│ logits = W.dot(vec) + b\n            # softmax\n            exp =            │
│ np.exp(logits - np.max(logits))\n            probs = exp / exp.sum()\n       │
│ pred = int(np.argmax(probs))\n            if pred == y:\n                    │
│ correct += 1\n            # gradient\n            probs[y] -= 1.0  # now     │
│ probs is (p - y)\n            gradW = probs[:, None] * vec[None, :]\n        │
│ gradb = probs\n            # update W and b\n            W -= lr * gradW\n   │
│ b -= lr * gradb\n            # update embeddings E for indices: dL/dE = (W^T │
│ * probs) * (1/len)\n            grad_vec = probs.dot(W)  # shape (dim,)\n    │
│ coeff = lr / max(1, len(inds))\n            for idx in inds:\n               │
│ E[idx] -= coeff * grad_vec\n        if (i//batch_size) % 50 == 0:\n          │
│ sys.stdout.write(&#x27;.&#x27;)\n            sys.stdout.flush()\n    acc =   │
│ correct/total if total>0 else 0.0\n    print(&#x27;\\nEpoch&#x27;, epoch+1,  │
│ &#x27;train_acc&#x27;, acc)\n\n    # Evaluate on test\n    tot = 0\n    corr │
│ = 0\n    for y, inds in test_samples:\n        vec = np.zeros((dim,),        │
│ dtype=np.float32)\n        for idx in inds:\n            vec += E[idx]\n     │
│ vec /= max(1, len(inds))\n        logits = W.dot(vec) + b\n        pred =    │
│ int(np.argmax(logits))\n        tot += 1\n        if pred == y: corr += 1\n  │
│ test_acc = corr / tot\n    print(&#x27;Test acc after epoch&#x27;, epoch+1,  │
│ &#x27;=&#x27;, test_acc)\n\n# Save model\nprint(&#x27;Saving model to&#x27;, │
│ model_path)\nnp.savez_compressed(model_path, W=W, b=b, E=E, buckets=buckets, │
│ dim=dim, num_classes=num_classes)\nsize =                                    │
│ os.path.getsize(model_path)\nprint(&#x27;Saved model size (MB):&#x27;,       │
│ size/1024/1024)\nprint(&#x27;Done&#x27;)\n","timeout":120}',                 │
│ name='CodeExecutor')] usage=RequestUsage(prompt_tokens=8433,                 │

│ hash_token(t):\n    return (hash(t) & 0xffffffff) % buckets\n\ndef           │
│ token_to_indices(token):\n    # word token\n    inds =                       │
│ [hash_token('w:'+token)]\n    # char ngrams\n    s = '<' + token + '>'\n     │
│ L = len(s)\n    for n in char_ngrams:\n        if L <= n: continue\n         │
│ for i in range(L - n + 1):\n            ng = s[i:i+n]\n                      │
│ inds.append(hash_token('g:'+ng))\n    return inds\n\ndef parse_line(line):\n │
│ # format: label<label> <text>\n\n    if not line: return None\n    parts =   │
│ line.split(' ',1)\n    if len(parts) < 2: return None\n    lbl =             │
│ parts[0].strip()\n    if not lbl.startswith('label'): return None\n    y =   │
│ int(lbl.replace('label',''))\n    text = parts[1].strip().lower()\n          │
│ tokens = text.split()\n    indices = []\n    for t in tokens:\n        t =   │
│ t.strip()\n        if not t: continue\n        inds = token_to_indices(t)\n  │
│ indices.extend(inds)\n    if len(indices) < min_tokens:\n        return      │

│ correct = 0\n    for i in range(0, len(train_samples), batch_size):\n        │
│ batch = train_samples[i:i+batch_size]\n        # process each sample in      │
│ batch\n        for y, inds in batch:\n            total += 1\n            #  │
│ compute averaged embedding\n            vec = np.zeros((dim,),               │
│ dtype=np.float32)\n            for idx in inds:\n                vec +=      │
│ E[idx]\n            vec /= max(1, len(inds))\n            # logits\n         │
│ logits = W.dot(vec) + b\n            # softmax\n            exp =            │
│ np.exp(logits - np.max(logits))\n            probs = exp / exp.sum()\n       │
│ pred = int(np.argmax(probs))\n            if pred == y:\n                    │
│ correct += 1\n            # gradient\n            probs[y] -= 1.0  # now     │
│ probs is (p - y)\n            gradW = probs[:, None] * vec[None, :]\n        │
│ gradb = probs\n            # update W and b\n            W -= lr * gradW\n   │
│ b -= lr * gradb\n            # update embeddings E for indices: dL/dE = (W^T │
│ * probs) * (1/len)\n            grad_vec = probs.dot(W)  # shape (dim,)\n    │
│ coeff = lr / max(1, len(inds))\n            for idx in inds:\n               │
│ E[idx] -= coeff * grad_vec\n        if (i//batch_size) % 50 == 0:\n          │
│ sys.stdout.write('.')\n            sys.stdout.flush()\n    acc =             │
│ correct/total if total>0 else 0.0\n    print('\nEpoch', epoch+1,             │
│ 'train_acc', acc)\n\n    # Evaluate on test\n    tot = 0\n    corr = 0\n     │
│ for y, inds in test_samples:\n        vec = np.zeros((dim,),                 │
│ dtype=np.float32)\n        for idx in inds:\n            vec += E[idx]\n     │
│ vec /= max(1, len(inds))\n        logits = W.dot(vec) + b\n        pred =    │
│ int(np.argmax(logits))\n        tot += 1\n        if pred == y: corr += 1\n  │
│ test_acc = corr / tot\n    print('Test acc after epoch', epoch+1, '=',       │
│ test_acc)\n\n# Save model\nprint('Saving model to',                          │

│ continue\n            y = int(lbl.replace('label',''))\n            text =   │

│ total += 1\n            # compute logit = sum W[:, idxs] + b\n            #  │
│ Use sum of columns\n            vec = W[:, idxs].sum(axis=1) / max(1,        │
│ len(idxs))\n            logits = vec + b\n            # softmax\n            │
│ mx = logits.max()\n            ex = np.exp(logits - mx)\n            probs = │
│ ex / ex.sum()\n            pred = int(np.argmax(probs))\n            if pred │
│ == y: correct += 1\n            # gradient\n            probs[y] -= 1.0\n    │
│ # update b\n            b -= lr * probs\n            # update W for          │
│ indices\n            grad = probs[:, None] * (1.0 / max(1, len(idxs)))\n     │
│ for idx in idxs:\n                W[:, idx] -= lr * grad[:,0]\n            # │
│ occasional progress\n            if total % 50000 == 0:\n                    │
│ print(f'  processed {total} samples, interim acc={correct/total:.4f}')\n     │
│ train_acc = correct/total if total>0 else 0.0\n    # evaluate\n    tot=0;    │
│ corr=0\n    for y, idxs in test:\n        vec = W[:, idxs].sum(axis=1) /     │
│ max(1, len(idxs))\n        logits = vec + b\n        pred =                  │
│ int(np.argmax(logits))\n        tot += 1\n        if pred == y: corr += 1\n  │
│ test_acc = corr / tot\n    print(f'Epoch {epoch} train_acc={train_acc:.4f}   │
│ test_acc={test_acc:.4f}')\n\n# save model\nprint('Saving model to',          │
│ model_path)\n# store as compressed numpy\nnp.savez_compressed(model_path,    │
│ W=W, b=b, D=D, num_classes=num_classes)\nprint('Saved model size MB=',       │
│ os.path.getsize(model_path)/1024/1024)\nPY", 'enter': True}                  │
